# Query 18: 0.98 QPS, 0.02x concurrency, ID 0x70556BCC1CC9CDA4F97A5FC725391EDB at byte 252824766
# This item is included in the report because it matches --limit.
# Scores: V/M = 0.01
# Time range: 2026-10-08T16:37:42 to 2026-10-08T16:38:36
# Attribute    pct   total     min     max     avg     95%  stddev  median
# ============ === ======= ======= ======= ======= ======= ======= =======
# Count          0      53
# Exec time      0      1s     7ms    78ms    23ms    44ms    13ms    20ms
# Lock time      0    39us       0     4us       0     1us       0     1us
# Rows sent     20 424.55k   7.46k   8.29k   8.01k   8.06k  275.65   8.06k
# Rows examine   4 556.75k   9.43k  11.16k  10.50k  10.80k  544.36  10.29k
# Query size     0  18.37k     355     355     355     355       0     355
# String:
# Databases    isupipe
# Hosts        localhost
# Users        isucon
# Query_time distribution
#   1us
#  10us
# 100us
#   1ms  ###########
#  10ms  ################################################################
# 100ms
#    1s
#  10s+
# Tables
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'livestreams'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`livestreams`\G
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'reactions'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`reactions`\G
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'livecomments'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`livecomments`\G
# EXPLAIN /*!50100 PARTITIONS*/
SELECT l.id AS livestreamid,
		IFNULL(r.reactions, 0) + IFNULL(c.tips, 0) AS score
		FROM livestreams l
		LEFT JOIN (SELECT livestream_id, COUNT(*) AS reactions FROM reactions
			GROUP BY livestream_id) r ON r.livestream_id = l.id
		LEFT JOIN (SELECT livestream_id, SUM(tip) AS tips FROM livecomments
			GROUP BY livestream_id) c ON c.livestream_id = l.id\G

# Query 19: 1.07 QPS, 0.02x concurrency, ID 0xA3A3D50430563E4324F17418B4E22A05 at byte 230084324
# This item is included in the report because it matches --limit.
# Scores: V/M = 0.01
# Time range: 2026-10-08T16:37:37 to 2026-10-08T16:38:34
# Attribute    pct   total     min     max     avg     95%  stddev  median
# ============ === ======= ======= ======= ======= ======= ======= =======
# Count          0      61
# Exec time      0   995ms     3ms    65ms    16ms    38ms    12ms    13ms
# Lock time      0    57us       0     6us       0     1us       0     1us
# Rows sent      3  81.52k   1.00k   1.68k   1.34k   1.61k  207.92   1.26k
# Rows examine   1 166.27k   2.25k   3.20k   2.73k   3.04k  301.32   2.62k
# Query size     0  25.79k     433     433     433     433       0     433
# String:
# Databases    isupipe
# Hosts        localhost
# Users        isucon
# Query_time distribution
#   1us
#  10us
# 100us
#   1ms  ####################################
#  10ms  ################################################################
# 100ms
#    1s
#  10s+
# Tables
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'users'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`users`\G
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'livestreams'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`livestreams`\G
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'reactions'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`reactions`\G
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'livecomments'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`livecomments`\G
# EXPLAIN /*!50100 PARTITIONS*/
SELECT u.name AS username,
		IFNULL(r.reactions, 0) + IFNULL(c.tips, 0) AS score
		FROM users u
		LEFT JOIN (SELECT l.user_id, COUNT(*) AS reactions
			FROM livestreams l INNER JOIN reactions r ON r.livestream_id = l.id
			GROUP BY l.user_id) r ON r.user_id = u.id
		LEFT JOIN (SELECT l.user_id, SUM(c.tip) AS tips
			FROM livestreams l INNER JOIN livecomments c ON c.livestream_id = l.id
			GROUP BY l.user_id) c ON c.user_id = u.id\G

# Tables
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'livecomments'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`livecomments`\G
#    SHOW TABLE STATUS FROM `isupipe` LIKE 'ng_words'\G
#    SHOW CREATE TABLE `isupipe`.`ng_words`\G
DELETE FROM livecomments WHERE livestream_id = 7674
		AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM ng_words WHERE livestream_id = 7674
			AND CONVERT(livecomments.comment USING utf8mb4) COLLATE utf8mb4_general_ci
			LIKE CONCAT('%', CONVERT(word USING utf8mb4) COLLATE utf8mb4_general_ci, '%'))\G
# Converted for EXPLAIN
# EXPLAIN /*!50100 PARTITIONS*/
select * from  livecomments WHERE livestream_id = 7674
		AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM ng_words WHERE livestream_id = 7674
			AND CONVERT(livecomments.comment USING utf8mb4) COLLATE utf8mb4_general_ci
			LIKE CONCAT('%', CONVERT(word USING utf8mb4) COLLATE utf8mb4_general_ci, '%'))\G
